## `AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Coaster-Column-Torque Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Amusement Ride Main Spindle Magnetic Particle & G-Force Accelerometer
- **Intro:** Coaster-Column-Torque Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Amusement Ride Main Spindle Magnetic Particle & G-Force Accelerometer — Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) and Zero-Energy Verification: Voltage at motor terminals: 0 VAC ± 0.1 VAC; spindle speed: 0 RPM ± 0.1 RPM.
- **Key tolerance:** Voltage at motor terminals: 0 VAC ± 0.1 VAC; spindle speed: 0 RPM ± 0.1 RPM.
- **Standards:** `ISO 9001:2015`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`, `ASTM E2627-13 Standard Practice for Magnetic Particle Testing`, `ANSI/ASSE Z244.1-2016 Control of Hazardous Energy - Lockout/Tago`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-9321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
